47 /**
48 * @brief `std::swap` is deleted for nda::basic_array_view.
49 * @warning The `std::swap` is WRONG for an nda::basic_array_view because of its copy/move semantics. Use nda::swap
50 * instead (the correct one, found by ADL).
51 */
52 template <typename V1, int R1, typename LP1, char A1, typename AP1, typename OP1, typename V2, int R2, typename LP2, char A2, typename AP2,
53 typename OP2>
54 void swap(nda::basic_array_view<V1, R1, LP1, A1, AP1, OP1> &a, nda::basic_array_view<V2, R2, LP2, A2, AP2, OP2> &b) = delete;
55

60 /**
61 * @ingroup arrays_views
62 * @brief A generic view of a multi-dimensional array.
63 *
64 * @details Together with nda::basic_array, this class forms the backbone of the nda library. It is templated with
65 * the following parameters:
66 *
67 * - `ValueType`: This is the type of the elements in the view. Depending on how the view was constructed and on the
68 * underlying array, this can be a const or non-const type.
69 * - `Rank`: Integer specifying the number of dimensions of the view. This is a compile-time constant.
70 * - `LayoutPolicy`: The layout policy specifies how the view accesses its elements. It provides a mapping from
71 * multi-dimensional to linear indices and vice versa (see @ref layout_pols).
72 * - `Algebra`: The algebra specifies how the view behaves when it is used in an expression. Possible values are 'A'
73 * (array), 'M' (matrix) and 'V' (vector) (see nda::get_algebra).
74 * - `AccessorPolicy`: The accessor policy specifies how the view accesses the data pointer. This can be useful for
75 * optimizations.
76 * - `OwningPolicy`: The owning policy specifies the ownership of the data pointer and who is responsible for
77 * handling the memory resources (see @ref mem_handles).
78 *
79 * In contrast to regular arrays (see nda::basic_array), views do not own the data they point to. They are a fast and
80 * efficient way to access and manipulate already existing data:
81 *
82 * @include doc_basic_array_view.cpp
83 *
84 * Output:
85 *
86 * ```
87 *
88 * [[1,1,1]
89 * [1,1,1]
90 * [1,1,1]]
91 *
92 * [[0,1,1]
93 * [0,1,1]
94 * [0,1,1]]
95 * ```
96 *
97 * Views are usually created by taking a slice of a regular nda::basic_array or another view. In the example above,
98 * `arr(nda::range::all, 0)` creates a view of the first column of the regular array `arr`, which is then set to zero.
99 * A view of the full array can be created with `arr()`.
100 *
101 * @tparam ValueType Type stored in the array.
102 * @tparam Rank Number of dimensions of the view.
103 * @tparam LayoutPolicy Policy determining the memory layout.
104 * @tparam Algebra Algebra of the view.
105 * @tparam AccessorPolicy Policy determining how the data pointer is accessed.
106 * @tparam OwningPolicy Policy determining the ownership of the data.
107 */
108 template <typename ValueType, int Rank, typename LayoutPolicy, char Algebra, typename AccessorPolicy, typename OwningPolicy>
109 class basic_array_view {
110 // Compile-time checks.
111 static_assert((Algebra != 'N'), "Internal error in nda::basic_array_view: Algebra 'N' not supported");
112 static_assert((Algebra != 'M') or (Rank == 2), "Internal error in nda::basic_array_view: Algebra 'M' requires a rank 2 view");
113 static_assert((Algebra != 'V') or (Rank == 1), "Internal error in nda::basic_array_view: Algebra 'V' requires a rank 1 view");
114
115 public:
116 /// Type of the values in the view (might be const).
117 using value_type = ValueType;
118
119 /// Type of the memory layout policy (see @ref layout_pols).
120 using layout_policy_t = LayoutPolicy;
121
122 /// Type of the memory layout (an nda::idx_map).
123 using layout_t = typename LayoutPolicy::template mapping<Rank>;
124
125 /// Type of the accessor policy (see e.g. nda::default_accessor).
126 using accessor_policy_t = AccessorPolicy;
127
128 /// Type of the owning policy (see @ref mem_pols).
129 using owning_policy_t = OwningPolicy;
130
131 /// Type of the memory handle (see @ref mem_handles).
132 using storage_t = typename OwningPolicy::template handle<ValueType>;
133
134 /// The associated regular (nda::basic_array) type.
135 using regular_type = basic_array<std::remove_const_t<ValueType>, Rank, C_layout, Algebra, heap<mem::get_addr_space<storage_t>>>;
136
137 /// Number of dimensions of the view.
138 static constexpr int rank = Rank;
139
140 // Compile-time check.
141 static_assert(not mem::have_device_compatible_addr_space<storage_t> or nda::have_device,
142 "Error in nda::basic_array_view: Device compatible address space requires compiling with device support.");
143
144 private:
145 // Type of the view itself.
146 using self_t = basic_array_view;
147
148 // Constexpr variable that is true if the view is a view (always for basic_array_view).
149 static constexpr bool is_view = true;
150
151 // Constexpr variable that is true if the value_type is const.
152 static constexpr bool is_const = std::is_const_v<ValueType>;
153
154 // Memory layout of the view, i.e. the nda::idx_map.
155 layout_t lay;
156
157 // Memory handle of the view.
158 storage_t sto;
159
160 // Declare any nda::basic_array as a friend.
161 template <typename T, int R, typename L, char A, typename CP>
162 friend class basic_array;
163
164 // Declare any other nda::basic_array_view as a friend.
165 template <typename T, int R, typename L, char A, typename AP, typename OP>
166 friend class basic_array_view;
167
168 // Check the layout compatibility of this view with another layout.
169 template <typename L>
170 static constexpr bool requires_runtime_check = not layout_property_compatible(L::template mapping<Rank>::layout_prop, layout_t::layout_prop);
171
172 public:
173 // FIXME : TRIQS PORTING
174 // private constructor for the previous friend
175 /**
176 * @brief Construct a view from a given layout and memory handle.
177 *
178 * @warning This should not be used directly. Use one of the other constructors instead.
179 *
180 * @param idxm Layout of the view.
181 * @param st Memory handle of the view.
182 */
183 99060 basic_array_view(layout_t const &idxm, storage_t st) : lay(idxm), sto(std::move(st)) {}
184
185 public:
186 // backward : FIXME : temporary to be removed
187 /// @deprecated Convert the current view to a view with an 'A' (array) algebra.
188 [[deprecated]] auto as_array_view() { return basic_array_view<ValueType, Rank, LayoutPolicy, 'A', AccessorPolicy, OwningPolicy>{*this}; };
189
190 /// @deprecated Convert the current view to a view with an 'A' (array) algebra.
191 [[deprecated]] auto as_array_view() const {
192 return basic_array_view<const ValueType, Rank, LayoutPolicy, 'A', AccessorPolicy, OwningPolicy>{*this};
193 };
194
195 /// Default constructor constructs an empty view with a default constructed memory handle and layout.
196 63 basic_array_view() = default;
197
198 /// Default move constructor moves the memory handle and layout.
199 basic_array_view(basic_array_view &&) = default;
200
201 /// Default copy constructor copies the memory handle and layout.
202 basic_array_view(basic_array_view const &) = default;
203
204 /**
205 * @brief Generic constructor from any nda::MemoryArray type.
206 *
207 * @details It simply copies the memory layout and initializes the memory handle with the handle of the given
208 * nda::MemoryArray object.
209 *
210 * @tparam A nda::MemoryArray type of the same rank as the view.
211 * @param a nda::MemoryArray object.
212 */
213 template <MemoryArrayOfRank<Rank> A>
214 requires((get_layout_info<A>.stride_order == layout_t::stride_order_encoded)
215 and (std::is_same_v<std::remove_const_t<ValueType>, get_value_t<A>>)
216 and (std::is_const_v<ValueType> or !std::is_const_v<typename std::decay_t<A>::value_type>))
217 explicit(requires_runtime_check<typename std::decay_t<A>::layout_policy_t>)
218 357 basic_array_view(A &&a) noexcept // NOLINT (should we forward the reference?)
219 357 : lay(a.indexmap()), sto(a.storage()) {}
220
221 /**
222 * @brief Construct a view from a bare pointer to some contiguous data and a shape.
223 *
224 * @note We do not have any control over the specified dimensions. The caller has to ensure their correctness and
225 * their compatibility with the given data pointer.
226 *
227 * @param shape Shape of the view.
228 * @param p Pointer to the data.
229 */
230 65 basic_array_view(std::array<long, Rank> const &shape, ValueType *p) noexcept : basic_array_view(layout_t{shape}, p) {}
231
232 /**
233 * @brief Construct a view from a bare pointer to some contiguous data and a memory layout.
234 *
235 * @note We do not have any control over the given layout. The caller has to ensure its correctness and its
236 * compatibility with the given data pointer.
237 *
238 * @param idxm Layout of the view.
239 * @param p Pointer to the data.
240 */
241 1369 basic_array_view(layout_t const &idxm, ValueType *p) noexcept : lay(idxm), sto{p} {}
242
243 /**
244 * @brief Construct a 1-dimensional view of a `std::array`.
245 *
246 * @tparam N Size of the `std::array`.
247 * @param a Reference to a `std::array` object.
248 */
249 template <size_t N>
250 requires(Rank == 1)
251 6 explicit basic_array_view(std::array<ValueType, N> &a) noexcept : basic_array_view{{long(N)}, a.data()} {}
252
253 /**
254 * @brief Construct a 1-dimensional view of a `std::array`.
255 *
256 * @tparam N Size of the `std::array`.
257 * @param a Const reference to a `std::array` object.
258 */
259 template <size_t N>
260 requires(Rank == 1 and std::is_const_v<ValueType>)
261 3 explicit basic_array_view(std::array<std::remove_const_t<ValueType>, N> const &a) noexcept : basic_array_view{{long(N)}, a.data()} {}
262
263 /**
264 * @brief Construct a 1-dimensional view of a general contiguous range.
265 *
266 * @tparam R Type of the range.
267 * @param rg Range object.
268 */
269 template <std::ranges::contiguous_range R>
270 requires(Rank == 1 and not MemoryArray<R>
271 and (std::is_same_v<std::ranges::range_value_t<R>, ValueType> or std::is_same_v<const std::ranges::range_value_t<R>, ValueType>))
272 50 explicit basic_array_view(R &rg) noexcept : basic_array_view{{long(std::ranges::size(rg))}, std::to_address(std::begin(rg))} {}
273
274 /**
275 * @brief Copy assignment operator makes a deep copy of the contents of the view.
276 *
277 * @details The dimension of the right hand side must be large enough or the behaviour is undefined. If
278 * `NDA_ENFORCE_BOUNDCHECK` is defined, bounds checking is enabled.
279 *
280 * @param rhs Right hand side of the assignment operation.
281 */
282 220 basic_array_view &operator=(basic_array_view const &rhs) noexcept {
283 220 assign_from_ndarray(rhs);
284 220 return *this;
285 }
286
287 /**
288 * @brief Assignment operator makes a deep copy of the contents of an nda::ArrayOfRank object.
289 *
290 * @details The dimension of the right hand side must be large enough or the behaviour is undefined. If
291 * `NDA_ENFORCE_BOUNDCHECK` is defined, bounds checking is enabled.
292 *
293 * @tparam RHS nda::ArrayOfRank type with the same rank as the view.
294 * @param rhs Right hand side of the assignment operation.
295 */
296 template <ArrayOfRank<Rank> RHS>
297 7551 basic_array_view &operator=(RHS const &rhs) noexcept {
298 static_assert(!is_const, "Cannot assign to an nda::basic_array_view with const value_type");
299 7551 assign_from_ndarray(rhs);
300 7551 return *this;
301 }
302
303 /**
304 * @brief Assignment operator assigns a scalar to the view.
305 *
306 * @details The behavior depends on the algebra of the view:
307 * - 'A' (array) and 'V' (vector): The scalar is assigned to all elements of the view.
308 * - 'M' (matrix): The scalar is assigned to the diagonal elements of the shorter dimension.
309 *
310 * @tparam RHS Type of the scalar.
311 * @param rhs Right hand side of the assignment operation.
312 */
313 template <typename RHS>
314 137 basic_array_view &operator=(RHS const &rhs) noexcept
315 requires(is_scalar_for_v<RHS, basic_array_view>)
316 {
317 static_assert(!is_const, "Cannot assign to an nda::basic_array_view with const value_type");
318 137 assign_from_scalar(rhs);
319 137 return *this;
320 }
321
322 /**
323 * @brief Assignment operator uses an nda::ArrayInitializer to assign to the view.
324 *
325 * @details The shape of the view is expected to be the same as the shape of the initializer.
326 *
327 * @tparam Initializer nda::ArrayInitializer type.
328 * @param initializer Initializer object.
329 */
330 template <ArrayInitializer<basic_array_view> Initializer>
331 9 basic_array_view &operator=(Initializer const &initializer) noexcept {
332 9 EXPECTS(shape() == initializer.shape());
333 9 initializer.invoke(*this);
334 9 return *this;
335 }
336
337 /**
338 * @brief Rebind the current view to another view.
339 *
340 * @details It simply copies the layout and memory handle of the other view.
341 *
342 * @tparam T Value type of the other view.
343 * @tparam R Rank of the other view.
344 * @tparam LP Layout policy of the other view.
345 * @tparam A Algebra of the other view.
346 * @tparam AP Accessor policy of the other view.
347 * @tparam OP Owning policy of the other view.
348 *
349 * @param v Other view.
350 */
351 template <typename T, int R, typename LP, char A, typename AP, typename OP>
352 7 void rebind(basic_array_view<T, R, LP, A, AP, OP> v) noexcept {
353 static_assert(R == Rank, "Cannot rebind a view to another view of a different rank");
354 static_assert(std::is_same_v<std::remove_const_t<T>, std::remove_const_t<ValueType>>,
355 "Cannot rebind a view to another view with a different value_type (except for const)");
356 static constexpr bool same_type = std::is_same_v<T, ValueType>;
357 static_assert(same_type or is_const, "Cannot rebind a view with non-const value_type to another view with const value_type");
358 if constexpr (same_type) {
359 // FIXME Error message in layout error !
360 6 lay = v.lay;
361 6 sto = v.sto;
362 } else if constexpr (is_const) {
363 // the last if is always trivially true but in case of an error in the static_assert above,
364 // it improves the error message by not compiling the = afterwards
365 1 lay = layout_t{v.indexmap()};
366 1 sto = storage_t{v.storage()};
367 }
368 7 }
369
370 /**
371 * @brief Swap two views by swapping their memory handles and layouts.
372 *
373 * @details This does not modify the data the views point to.
374 *
375 * @param a First view.
376 * @param b Second view.
377 */
378 1 friend void swap(basic_array_view &a, basic_array_view &b) noexcept {
379 1 std::swap(a.lay, b.lay);
380 1 std::swap(a.sto, b.sto);
381 1 }
382
383 /**
384 * @brief Swap two views by swapping their data.
385 *
386 * @details This modifies the data the views point to.
387 *
388 * @param a First view.
389 * @param b Second view.
390 */
391 34 friend void deep_swap(basic_array_view a, basic_array_view b) noexcept {
392 34 auto tmp = make_regular(a);
393 34 a = b;
394 34 b = tmp;
395 34 }
396
397 // include common functionality of arrays and views
398 #include "./_impl_basic_array_view_common.hpp"
399 };

404 -> basic_array_view<std::conditional_t<std::is_const_v<std::remove_reference_t<A>>, const typename std::decay_t<A>::value_type,
405 typename std::decay_t<A>::value_type>,
406 get_rank<A>, typename std::decay_t<A>::layout_policy_t, get_algebra<A>, default_accessor, borrowed<mem::get_addr_space<A>>>;

411 default_accessor, borrowed<>>;
